).

Appendix E
Proof of
Corollary

1

The proof is straight-forward by noting that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{x_{t}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}}\Bigg[\sum_{y\neq x_{t}}\hat{R}_{t}(x_{t},y)-\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x_{t},y)+\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x_{t},y)\log\frac{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x_{t},y)}{\hat{R}_{t}(x_{t},y)}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{x_{t}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}}\Bigg[\sum_{y\neq x_{t}}R_{T-t}(y,x_{t})s_{T-t}(y,x_{t})-R_{T-t}(y,x_{t})\frac{q_{T-t}(y)}{q_{T-t}(x_{t})}

\displaystyle\qquad+R_{T-t}(y,x_{t})\frac{q_{T-t}(y)}{q_{T-t}(x_{t})}\log\frac{R_{T-t}(y,x_{t})(q_{T-t}(y)/q_{T-t}(x_{t}))}{R_{T-t}(y,x_{t})s_{T-t}(y,x_{t})}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{x_{t}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}}\Bigg[\sum_{y\neq x_{t}}R_{T-t}(y,x_{t})\left(s_{T-t}(y,x_{t})-\frac{q_{T-t}(y)}{q_{T-t}(x_{t})}+\frac{q_{T-t}(y)}{q_{T-t}(x_{t})}\log\frac{q_{T-t}(y)/q_{T-t}(x_{t})}{s_{T-t}(y,x_{t})}\right)\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathcal{L}_{SE}(T-t;s_{T-t})

where the last line follows from the definition of score-entropy in (
4
).

Appendix F
Proof of
Theorem

2

In this section, we provide the proof of
Theorem

2
. Before we start, the following assumption characterizes those general approximate deterministic-step-size samplers (i.e., approximation to the Kolmogorov samplers) that can sample efficiently.

Definition 1

(Approximate Sampling Method)
.

The sampling rate
\hat{R}_{t}
is piecewise constant, i.e., constant within
t\in[t_{k},t_{k+1})
. Also, given
x_{t_{k}}\in[S]^{d}
, we have
\hat{R}_{t}(x_{t_{k}},\cdot)=\hat{R}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},\cdot)
.

Definition

1
is especially useful for discrete diffusion models where the exact solution of the Kolmogorov equation of the sampling CTMC is computationally hard to obtain. In particular,
Definition

1
is satisfied for the rate of
\tau
-leaping (see (
7
)). It will also be satisfied for the truncated
\tau
-leaping method later (see (
30
)).

F.1
Step 1: Decomposing total error

To begin, we can employ the general result of
Theorem

1
and get that

\displaystyle\mathrm{KL}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{N}}||p_{t_{N}})

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{KL}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0}||p_{0})+\sum_{k=0}^{N-1}\mathbb{E}_{x_{t_{k}}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{k}}\left[\mathrm{KL}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\cdot|x_{t_{k}})||p_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\cdot|x_{t_{k}}))\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{KL}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}}\underbrace{\left[\sum_{y\neq x_{t}}\hat{R}_{t}(x_{t},y)-\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x_{t},y)}{\hat{R}_{t}(x_{t},y)}\right]}_{=:g_{t}(x_{t})}\mathrm{d}t.

(26)

Note that
g_{t}
is a Bregman divergence (generated by the negative entropy function) for each
x
, and thus
g_{t}(x)\geq 0
for all
x\in[S]^{d}
. To see this, we fix
x_{t}
and consider two vectors
p
and
q
such that
p_{y}:=\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x_{t},y)
and
q_{y}:=\hat{R}_{t}(x_{t},y)
such that
y\neq x_{t}
. Also, let
\phi(p):=\sum_{y\neq x_{t}}p_{y}\log p_{y}
(i.e., the negative entropy function, which is convex), and the corresponding Bregman divergence is
D_{\phi}(p,q)=\phi(p)-\phi(q)-\langle y-x,\phi(y)\rangle=\sum_{y\neq x_{t}}p_{y}\log p_{y}-q_{y}\log q_{y}-(p_{y}-q_{y})(1+\log q_{y})=\sum_{y\neq x_{t}}p_{y}\log(p_{y}/q_{y})-p_{y}+q_{y}
, which is exactly
g_{t}
. We further decompose
g_{t}
into three different terms:

\displaystyle\mathrm{KL}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{T}||p_{T})\leq\mathrm{KL}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0}||p_{0})+\sum_{k=0}^{N-1}\int_{t_{k}}^{t_{k+1}}\mathbb{E}_{x_{t}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}}\left[g_{t}(x_{t})\right]\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathrm{KL}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0}||p_{0})}_{\text{initialization error}}+\underbrace{\sum_{k=0}^{N-1}(t_{k+1}-t_{k})\mathbb{E}_{x_{t_{k}}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}}\left[g_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}})\right]}_{\text{estimation error}}+

\displaystyle\qquad\underbrace{\sum_{k=0}^{N-1}\int_{t_{k}}^{t_{k+1}}\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}x_{t}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}\\
x_{t_{k}}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}\end{subarray}}\left[g_{t}(x_{t})-g_{t}(x_{t_{k}})\right]+\mathbb{E}_{x_{t_{k}}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}}\left[g_{t}(x_{t_{k}})-g_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}})\right]\mathrm{d}t}_{\text{discretization error}}.

(27)

From
[
19
, Proposition 2]
and
[
18
, Theorem C.1]
, the initialization error satisfies that

\mathrm{KL}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0}||p_{0})\lesssim(d\log S)e^{-T}.

Recall (
3
) and (
5
). Note that the estimation error term can be upper-bounded as

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{N-1}(t_{k+1}-t_{k})\mathbb{E}_{x_{t_{k}}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}}\left[g_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}})\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=0}^{N-1}(t_{k+1}-t_{k})\mathbb{E}_{x_{t_{k}}\sim q_{T-t_{k}}}\left[\sum_{y\neq x_{t_{k}}}\hat{R}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)-\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)+\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)\log\frac{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)}{\hat{R}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=0}^{N-1}(t_{k+1}-t_{k})\mathbb{E}_{x_{t_{k}}\sim q_{T-t_{k}}}\sum_{y\neq x_{t_{k}}}R_{T-t_{k}}(y,x_{t_{k}})\times

\displaystyle\qquad\left(s_{T-t_{k}}(y,x_{t_{k}})-\frac{q_{T-t_{k}}(y)}{q_{T-t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}})}+\frac{q_{T-t_{k}}(y)}{q_{T-t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}})}\log\frac{q_{T-t_{k}}(y)/q_{T-t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}})}{s_{T-t_{k}}(y,x_{t_{k}})}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=0}^{N-1}(t_{k+1}-t_{k})\mathcal{L}_{SE}(T-t_{k};s_{T-t_{k}})

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon_{\text{score}},

where the last line follows from
1
. As follows, the goal is to provide an upper bound for the discretization error.

F.2
Step 2: Identifying dominant term for discretization error

As shown in (
F.1
), the discretization error can be decomposed into two terms, one for the time-difference in the argument of
g_{t}
(in expected value), and the other for the difference in
g_{t}
itself. In the following lemma, we show that the former term decays faster than the other, which further implies that the latter term is the dominant error term for the discretization error.

Lemma 1
.

For each
k=0,\dots,N-1
and
t\in[t_{k},t_{k+1})
, We have

\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}x_{t}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}\\
x_{t_{k}}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}\end{subarray}}\left[g_{t}(x_{t})-g_{t}(x_{t_{k}})\right]\lesssim(t-t_{k})d\cdot\mathbb{E}_{x_{t}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}}\left[g_{t}(x_{t})\right].

Proof.

See
Section

H.1
.
∎

As a result of
Lemma

1
, suppose that
t_{k+1}-t_{k}\leq\kappa
, we further have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T-\delta}\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}x_{t}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}\\
x_{t_{k}}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}\end{subarray}}\left[g_{t}(x_{t})-g_{t}(x_{t_{k}})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\kappa\cdot O\left(\int_{0}^{T-\delta}\mathbb{E}_{x_{t}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}}\left[g_{t}(x_{t})\right]\mathrm{d}t\right)

\displaystyle=\kappa\cdot O\left(\varepsilon_{\text{score}}+\int_{0}^{T-\delta}\mathbb{E}_{x_{t_{k}}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}}\left[g_{t}(x_{t_{k}})-g_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}})\right]\mathrm{d}t\right).

(28)

Here the last line follows from the decomposition of
g_{t}
as in (
F.1
).
Thus, this term (corresponding to the difference in
x_{t}
in expectation) does not contribute to the overall rate as long as
\kappa\to 0
.

F.3
Step 3: Bounding dominant term for discretization error

Now, we control the second term in the discretization error in (
F.1
), which is also the dominant error term as shown in Step 2. We also explicitly express its parameter dependencies. The following lemma provides a useful score bound for further analysis, which is similar to
[
18
, Remark B.3]
and
[
19
, Lemma 2]
and provided here for completeness.

Lemma 2
.

Fix
t>0
and
x\neq y
such that
\mathrm{Ham}\left(x,y\right)=1
. Given the forward process in (
1
) with a rate given in (
2
), we have

\frac{q_{t}(y)}{q_{t}(x)}\lesssim S\cdot\max\{1,t^{-1}\}.

Proof.

See
Section

H.2
.
∎

Now, we can upper-bound the error due to difference in
g_{t}
as a difference in the likelihood ratio, as shown in the following lemma.

Lemma 3
.

Fix
t\in[t_{k},t_{k+1})
. Under
2
and
1
, we have

\mathbb{E}_{x_{t_{k}}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}}\left(g_{t}(x_{t_{k}})-g_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}})\right)\lesssim(1+\log(MS\delta^{-1}))\mathbb{E}_{x_{t_{k}}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}}\sum_{y\neq x_{t_{k}}}\left|\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x_{t_{k}},y)-\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)\right|.

Proof.

See
Section

H.3
.
∎

Now, for any
x_{t_{k}}\in[S]^{d}
, the sum difference in the reverse rate can be further calculated using (
3
) as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{x_{t_{k}}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}}\sum_{y\neq x_{t_{k}}}\left|\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)-\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x_{t_{k}},y)\right|

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{x_{t_{k}}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}}\sum_{y\neq x_{t_{k}}}\left|\frac{q_{T-t_{k}}(y)}{q_{T-t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}})}R_{T-t_{k}}(y,x_{t_{k}})-\frac{q_{T-t}(y)}{q_{T-t}(x_{t_{k}})}R_{T-t}(y,x_{t_{k}})\right|

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{x_{t_{k}}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\neq x_{t_{k}}\\
\mathrm{Ham}\left(y,x_{t_{k}}\right)=1\end{subarray}}\left|\frac{q_{T-t_{k}}(y)}{q_{T-t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}})}-\frac{q_{T-t}(y)}{q_{T-t}(x_{t_{k}})}\right|R_{T-t_{k}}(y,x_{t_{k}})

where the last line follows because
R_{T-t_{k}}(y,x_{t_{k}})=R_{T-t}(y,x_{t_{k}})
whenever
y\neq x_{t_{k}}
since
\beta_{t}\equiv 1
.

Due to continuity of this ratio (i.e., the concrete score), one common way is to upper bound its derivative uniformly for every fixed
x
and
y
such that
\mathrm{Ham}\left(x,y\right)=1
. Indeed, this is the approach taken by
[
18
, Proposition C.2]
(cf.
[
9
, Proposition 6]
). For reasons of comparison, the following upper-bound adopts the derivative-based method as in
[
18
, Proposition C.2]
.

Lemma 4

(Following the idea in
[
18
]
)
.

Fix
s<t
such that
t-s
is small. Fix
x
and
y
such that
\mathrm{Ham}\left(x,y\right)=1
. Given the forward process in (
1
) with a rate given in (
2
), we have

\left|\frac{q_{t}(y)}{q_{t}(x)}-\frac{q_{s}(y)}{q_{s}(x)}\right|\lesssim dS^{2}\max\left\{1,s^{-2}\right\}(t-s).

This further implies that

\mathbb{E}_{x_{t_{k}}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\neq x_{t_{k}}\\
\mathrm{Ham}\left(y,x_{t_{k}}\right)=1\end{subarray}}\left|\frac{q_{T-t_{k}}(y)}{q_{T-t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}})}-\frac{q_{T-t}(y)}{q_{T-t}(x_{t_{k}})}\right|R_{T-t_{k}}(y,x_{t_{k}})\\
\lesssim d^{2}S^{2}\max\left\{1,(T-t_{k+1})^{-2}\right\}(t-t_{k}).

Proof.

See
Section

H.4
.
∎

Then, we present our novel approach below that directly provides an upper bound in expectation. This will finally result in a tighter upper bound with linear
S
dependency.

Lemma 5
.

Fix
s<t
such that
t-s
is small. Given the forward process in (
1
) with a rate given in (
2
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{x_{t}\sim q_{t}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\neq x_{t}\\
\mathrm{Ham}\left(y,x_{t}\right)=1\end{subarray}}\left|\frac{q_{t}(y)}{q_{t}(x_{t})}-\frac{q_{s}(y)}{q_{s}(x_{t})}\right|

\displaystyle R_{t}(y,x_{t})

\displaystyle\lesssim dS\max\{1,s^{-2}\}(t-s)+d^{2}S\max\{1,s^{-1}\}(t-s)

\displaystyle\lesssim d^{2}S\max\{1,s^{-2}\}(t-s).

Proof.

See
Section

H.5
.
∎

Thus, considering the two terms in (
F.3
), we have the following bound for the expected difference in the reverse rate matrix:

\mathbb{E}_{x_{t_{k}}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}}\sum_{y\neq x_{t_{k}}}\left|\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)-\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x_{t_{k}},y)\right|\lesssim d^{2}S\max\{1,(T-t_{k+1})^{-2}\}(t-t_{k}).

Collecting the results from Steps 1–3, we would arrive at

\displaystyle\mathrm{KL}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{N}}||p_{t_{N}})

\displaystyle\lesssim d(\log S)e^{-T}+\varepsilon_{\text{score}}+d^{2}S(1+\log(MS\delta^{-1}))\sum_{k=0}^{N-1}\max\{1,(T-t_{k+1})^{-2}\}(t_{k+1}-t_{k})^{2}.

Finally, to determine the overall parameter dependencies in the above summation, we can consider the particular step-size:
t_{k+1}-t_{k}=\kappa\min\left\{1,T-t_{k}\right\}
and invoke
[
24
, Lemma 18]
, which shows that

\sum_{k=0}^{N-1}\max\{1,(T-t_{k+1})^{-2}\}(t_{k+1}-t_{k})^{2}\lesssim\kappa(T+\log\delta^{-1}).

Also, from the last part of
[
17
, Theorem 6]
, the perturbation due to early-stopping is

\mathrm{TV}(q_{0},q_{\delta})\lesssim d\delta,\quad\text{as}\penalty\ \delta\to 0.

The proof for
Theorem

2
is complete.

Appendix G
Proof of
Theorem

3

The proof of
Theorem

3
consists of three parts. First, we construct a non-trivial approximate discrete sampler, the truncated
\tau
-leaping algorithm, with explicit intermediate rate that allows for categorical per-step sampling. Then, we show that our truncated
\tau
-leaping is asymptotically equivalent to both the Euler method and Tweedie
\tau
-leaping in terms of the categorical sampling probabilities. Finally, we show that our proof of
Theorem

2
is applicable even for such asumptotically equivalent samplers, which is further evidence of the generality of our approach.

G.1
Step 1: Constructing an approximate sampler

To start, we propose an approximate discrete sampler that modifies the vanilla
\tau
-leaping algorithm and enables categorical sampling. We call this sampler the
truncated
\tau
-leaping
algorithm (see
Appendix

C
). The intuition is that we only allow the first state change (a.k.a. truncated) for each dimension according to (
7
).
This intuition is made solid by the following proposition, which shows explicitly the rate of truncated
\tau
-leaping.

Lemma 6
.

Fix
k\in\{0,\dots,N-1\}
and
x_{t_{k}}\in[S]^{d}
. The truncated
\tau
-leaping algorithm corresponds to the following rate matrix:
\forall t\in[t_{k},t_{k+1})
and
\forall(x,y):x\neq y
,

\hat{R}^{\text{TTL}}_{t}(x,y):=\hat{R}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y-x+x_{t_{k}})\mathds{1}\left\{\mathrm{Ham}\left(x,y\right)=1,\mathrm{nzind}(y-x)\in\mathrm{zeros}(x-x_{t_{k}})\right\},

(30)

where
\mathrm{nzind}(y-x)
is the only index
i^{*}
such that
x^{i^{*}}\neq y^{i^{*}}
, and
\mathrm{zeros}(v):=\{i:v^{i}=0\}
is the set of indices having zeros in a vector
v\in[S]^{d}
.

Proof.

See
Section

H.6
.
∎

G.2
Step 2: Establishing asymptotic equivalency

Then, we show that both the Euler method and Tweedie
\tau
-leaping are (first-order) asymptotically equivalent to truncated
\tau
-leaping. This is summarized in the following proposition.

Lemma 7
.

Fix
t_{k}\in[0,T-\delta]
,
x_{t_{k}}\in[S]^{d}
, and
i\in[d]
. With some abuse of notation, define
P_{\text{truncated}}^{i}(a)
,
P_{\text{tweedie}}^{i}(a)
, and
P_{\text{euler}}^{i}(a)
as the conditional probability of
x_{t_{k+1}}^{i}=a
given
x_{t_{k}}
for these three algorithms, respectively. Then, as
t_{k+1}-t_{k}\to 0
,

P_{\text{truncated}}^{i}(a)=P_{\text{euler}}^{i}(a)(1+o(1))=P_{\text{tweedie}}^{i}(a)(1+o(1)),\penalty\ \forall a\in[S].

Proof.

See
Section

H.7
.
∎

In the context of
Theorem

3
, note that
t_{k+1}-t_{k}\leq\kappa
. Thus,
Lemma

7
shows that the constructed truncated
\tau
-leaping is asymptotically equivalent to both the Euler method and Tweedie
\tau
-leaping when
\kappa\to 0
(or equivalently, when
N\to\infty
).

G.3
Step 3: Examining the error induced by asymptotically equivalent samplers

Let
p_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}
denote the (exact) conditional probability from truncated
\tau
-leaping, and let
p^{\prime}_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}
be any conditional probability such that
p^{\prime}_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\cdot|x_{t_{k}})=p_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\cdot|x_{t_{k}})(1+o(1))
for fixed
x_{t_{k}}\in[S]^{d}
, as
\kappa\to 0
. Previously, from
Lemma

7
, we have shown that both Euler method and Tweedie
\tau
-leaping are special cases of such
p^{\prime}_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}
.

A useful property for such
p^{\prime}_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}
is that, for fixed
x_{t_{k}}\in[S]^{d}
, as
t_{k+1}-t_{k}\to 0
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{KL}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\cdot|x_{t_{k}})||p^{\prime}_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\cdot|x_{t_{k}}))

\displaystyle=\sum_{\tilde{x}\in[S]^{d}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\tilde{x}|x_{t_{k}})\log\frac{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\tilde{x}|x_{t_{k}})}{p^{\prime}_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\tilde{x}|x_{t_{k}})}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\tilde{x}\in[S]^{d}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\tilde{x}|x_{t_{k}})\log\frac{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\tilde{x}|x_{t_{k}})}{p_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\tilde{x}|x_{t_{k}})}+\sum_{\tilde{x}\in[S]^{d}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\tilde{x}|x_{t_{k}})\log\frac{1}{1+o(1)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\tilde{x}\in[S]^{d}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\tilde{x}|x_{t_{k}})\log\frac{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\tilde{x}|x_{t_{k}})}{p_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\tilde{x}|x_{t_{k}})}-\sum_{\tilde{x}\in[S]^{d}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\tilde{x}|x_{t_{k}})o(1)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\tilde{x}\in[S]^{d}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\tilde{x}|x_{t_{k}})\log\frac{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\tilde{x}|x_{t_{k}})}{p_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\tilde{x}|x_{t_{k}})}+o(1).

(31)

Now we consider the decomposition in (
F.1
). We have

\displaystyle\mathrm{KL}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{N}}||p^{\prime}_{t_{N}})

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{KL}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0}||p_{0})+\sum_{k=0}^{N-1}\mathbb{E}_{x_{t_{k}}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{k}}\left[\mathrm{KL}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\cdot|x_{t_{k}})||p^{\prime}_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\cdot|x_{t_{k}}))\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathrm{KL}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0}||p_{0})+\sum_{k=0}^{N-1}\mathbb{E}_{x_{t_{k}}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{k}}\left[\mathrm{KL}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\cdot|x_{t_{k}})||p_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}(\cdot|x_{t_{k}}))\right]

where the last line follows from (
G.3
). Thus we have recovered the result in (
F.1
).

G.4
Step 4: Examining the rate of truncated
\tau
-leaping

Now, we can verify that the rate matrix in (
30
) satisfies
Definition

1
, just as vanilla
\tau
-leaping. Thus, the rate of
Theorem

2
still holds if we substitute
\tau
-leaping with truncated
\tau
-leaping. The proof of
Theorem

3
is now complete.

Appendix H
Proofs of Auxiliary Lemmas

H.1
Proof of
Lemma

1

With the forward process in (
1
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}x_{t}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}\\
x_{t_{k}}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}\end{subarray}}\left[g_{t}(x_{t})-g_{t}(x_{t_{k}})\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{x_{t}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}}\left[g_{t}(x_{t})-\sum_{x_{t_{k}}\in[S]^{d}}q_{T-t_{k}|T-t}(x_{t_{k}}|x_{t})g_{t}(x_{t_{k}})\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{x_{t}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}}\left[g_{t}(x_{t})-\sum_{x_{t_{k}}\in[S]^{d}}\left(\mathds{1}\left\{x_{t_{k}}=x_{t}\right\}+R_{t}(x_{t},x_{t_{k}})(t-t_{k})\right)g_{t}(x_{t_{k}})\right]+o(t-t_{k})

\displaystyle=(t-t_{k})\mathbb{E}_{x_{t}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}}\left[-\sum_{x_{t_{k}}\in[S]^{d}}R_{t}(x_{t},x_{t_{k}})g_{t}(x_{t_{k}})\right]+o(t-t_{k})

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{\leq}}(t-t_{k})\mathbb{E}_{x_{t}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}}\left[(-R_{t}(x_{t},x_{t}))g_{t}(x_{t})\right]+o(t-t_{k})

\displaystyle=(t-t_{k})\frac{S-1}{S}d\cdot\mathbb{E}_{x_{t}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}}\left[g_{t}(x_{t})\right]+o(t-t_{k})

where
(i)
follows because
g_{t}(x)\geq 0
and
R_{t}(x,y)\geq 0
if
x\neq y
. Also, for the last line, note that
R_{t}(x,x)=-\sum_{y\neq x}R_{t}(x,y)=-\frac{S-1}{S}d
when
\beta_{t}\equiv 1
. The proof is now complete.

H.2
Proof of
Lemma

2

Let
j
be the only index such that
x^{j}\neq y^{j}
. First, we note that

\displaystyle\frac{q_{t}(y)}{q_{t}(x)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{q_{t}(x)}\sum_{x_{0}\in[S]^{d}}q_{0}(x_{0})q_{t|0}(y|x_{0})

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{=}}\frac{1}{q_{t}(x)}\sum_{x_{0}\in[S]^{d}}q_{0}(x_{0})\prod_{i\in[d]}q_{t|0}^{i}(y^{i}|x_{0}^{i})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{q_{t}(x)}\sum_{x_{0}\in[S]^{d}}q_{0}(x_{0})\left(\prod_{i\in[d]}q_{t|0}^{i}(x^{i}|x_{0}^{i})\right)\left(\frac{q_{t|0}^{j}(y^{j}|x_{0}^{j})}{q_{t|0}^{j}(x^{j}|x_{0}^{j})}\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(ii)}}{{=}}\sum_{x_{0}\in[S]^{d}}\frac{q_{0}(x_{0})q_{t|0}(x|x_{0})}{q_{t}(x)}\left(\frac{q_{t|0}^{j}(y^{j}|x_{0}^{j})}{q_{t|0}^{j}(x^{j}|x_{0}^{j})}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{x_{0}\sim q_{0|t}(\cdot|x)}\frac{q^{j}_{t|0}(y^{j}|x_{0}^{j})}{q^{j}_{t|0}(x^{j}|x_{0}^{j})}

(32)

where both
(i)
and
(ii)
follow because with the chosen
R_{t}
in (
2
) each dimension propagates independently in the forward process (cf.
[
9
, Prop. 3]
). Note that the reverse process does not propagate independently.

To obtain an analytical solution for the conditional probability, we can solve the Kolmogorov forward equation for the
i
-th dimension (
\forall i\in[d]
) (cf.
[
19
, Proposition 1]
):

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}q^{i}_{t|0}(z|a)=\sum_{\tilde{z}\in[S]}q^{i}_{t|0}(\tilde{z}|a)R^{\text{tok}}_{t}(\tilde{z},z),

whose solution is

\displaystyle q^{i}_{t|0}(z|a)

\displaystyle=\left[\exp\left(\int_{0}^{t}R^{\text{tok}}_{s}\mathrm{d}s\right)\right](a,z)=\left[\exp\left(tR_{\text{base}}\right)\right](a,z)=\left[P\exp\left(t\Lambda\right)P^{-1}\right](a,z)

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}S^{-1}(1-e^{-t})&\text{if}\penalty\ z\neq a\\
S^{-1}(1+(S-1)e^{-t})&\text{if}\penalty\ z=a\end{cases}

(33)

where we recall that
R^{\text{tok}}_{t}=R_{\text{base}}
when
\beta_{t}\equiv 1
and we denote the eigendecomposition of
R_{\text{base}}=S^{-1}\bm{1}_{S}\bm{1}_{S}^{\intercal}-I_{S}
as
R_{\text{base}}=P\Lambda P^{-1}
.
Thus, plugging back into (
H.2
), we have

\frac{q^{j}_{t|0}(y^{j}|x_{0}^{j})}{q^{j}_{t|0}(x^{j}|x_{0}^{j})}=\begin{cases}1&\text{if}\penalty\ x^{j}\neq x_{0}^{j}\penalty\ \text{and}\penalty\ y^{j}\neq x_{0}^{j}\\
\frac{1-e^{-t}}{1+(S-1)e^{-t}}&\text{if}\penalty\ x^{j}=x_{0}^{j}\penalty\ \text{but}\penalty\ y^{j}\neq x_{0}^{j}\\
\frac{1+(S-1)e^{-t}}{1-e^{-t}}&\text{if}\penalty\ x^{j}\neq x_{0}^{j}\penalty\ \text{but}\penalty\ y^{j}=x_{0}^{j}\end{cases}.

(34)

Among the three cases above, since
e^{-t}\geq 0
, the second case satisfies that
\frac{1-e^{-t}}{1+(S-1)e^{-t}}\leq 1
. Also, the third case satisfies that

\displaystyle\frac{1+(S-1)e^{-t}}{1-e^{-t}}

\displaystyle=1+S\cdot\frac{1}{e^{t}-1}\leq\begin{cases}S+1&\text{if}\penalty\ e^{t}\geq 2\\
\frac{S}{t}&\text{otherwise}\end{cases}

\displaystyle\lesssim S\cdot\max\{1,t^{-1}\}.

Therefore, the bound is as desired.

H.3
Proof of
Lemma

3

By definition of
g_{t}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{x_{t_{k}}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}}\left(g_{t}(x_{t_{k}})-g_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}})\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{x_{t_{k}}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}}\sum_{y\neq x_{t_{k}}}\left(\hat{R}_{t}(x_{t_{k}},y)-\hat{R}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)\right)-\left(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x_{t_{k}},y)-\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x_{t_{k}},y)\log\frac{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x_{t_{k}},y)}{\hat{R}_{t}(x_{t_{k}},y)}-\mathchoice{\mkern \displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{=}}\mathbb{E}_{x_{t_{k}}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern
