2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)}{\hat{R}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad-\left(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x_{t_{k}},y)-\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{x_{t_{k}}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}}\sum_{y\neq x_{t_{k}}}\left|\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x_{t_{k}},y)-\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)\right|\left(1+\left|\log\hat{R}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)\right|\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left|\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x_{t_{k}},y)\log\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x_{t_{k}},y)-\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)\log\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)\right|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(ii)}}{{\lesssim}}(1+\log(MS\delta^{-1}))\cdot\mathbb{E}_{x_{t_{k}}\sim\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}}\sum_{y\neq x_{t_{k}}}\left|\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x_{t_{k}},y)-\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)\right|

where
(i)
follows by
Definition

1
. We explain
(ii)
as follows. First, from (
5
), if
x\neq y
,

\hat{R}_{t_{k}}(x,y)=R_{T-t_{k}}(y,x)s_{T-t_{k}}(y,x)\in[M^{-1}S^{-1},MS^{-1}]

under
2
, which further implies that
\left|\log\hat{R}_{t_{k}}(x,y)\right|\leq\log(MS)
. Also,

\displaystyle\left|\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x_{t_{k}},y)\log\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x_{t_{k}},y)-\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)\log\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)\right|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(iii)}}{{\leq}}\left(1+\left|\log\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}^{*}\right|\right)\left|\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x_{t_{k}},y)-\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)\right|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(iv)}}{{\lesssim}}\left(1+\log(S\delta^{-1})\right)\left|\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x_{t_{k}},y)-\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)\right|

where
(iii)
follows from the intermediate-value theorem for
f(z)=z\log z
and
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}^{*}
is a number between
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)
and
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x_{t_{k}},y)
, and
(iv)
follows because, by
Lemma

2
,
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x,y)=R_{T-t}(y,x)\frac{q_{T-t}(y)}{q_{T-t}(x)}\lesssim\max\{1,(T-t)^{-1}\}\leq\delta^{-1}
for all
t>0
if
x\neq y
. Meanwhile, by symmetry,
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x,y)\gtrsim\frac{1}{S^{2}\max\{1,(T-t)^{-1}\}}\geq\frac{\delta}{S^{2}}
. The proof is now complete.

H.4
Proof of
Lemma

4

The idea comes from
[
18
, Proposition C.2]
. When
t-s
is small, we note that the derivative of the likelihood ratio w.r.t.
t
is equal to

\left|\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\left(\frac{q_{t}(y)}{q_{t}(x)}\right)\right|=\left|\frac{\frac{\partial}{\partial t}q_{t}(y)}{q_{t}(x)}-\frac{q_{t}(y)\frac{\partial}{\partial t}q_{t}(x)}{q_{t}(x)^{2}}\right|\leq\frac{\left|\frac{\partial}{\partial t}q_{t}(y)\right|}{q_{t}(x)}+\frac{q_{t}(y)\left|\frac{\partial}{\partial t}q_{t}(x)\right|}{q_{t}(x)^{2}}.

Now, by Kolmogorov forward equation,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\frac{\partial}{\partial t}q_{t}(y)}{q_{t}(x)}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\sum_{y^{\prime}\in[S]^{d}}\frac{q_{t}(y^{\prime})}{q_{t}(x)}R_{t}(y^{\prime},y)\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{S}\cdot\frac{q_{t}(y)}{q_{t}(x)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y^{\prime}\in[S]^{d}\\
\mathrm{Ham}\left(y,y^{\prime}\right)=1\end{subarray}}\frac{q_{t}(y^{\prime})}{q_{t}(y)}+d\frac{S-1}{S}\frac{q_{t}(y)}{q_{t}(x)}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{\lesssim}}dS^{2}\max\{1,t^{-1}\}^{2}+dS\max\{1,t^{-1}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim dS^{2}\max\{1,t^{-1}\}^{2}

where
(i)
follows from
Lemma

2
and note that the summation has
d(S-1)
terms in total. With a similar argument, we have

\left|\frac{\frac{\partial}{\partial t}q_{t}(x)}{q_{t}(x)}\right|=\frac{1}{S}\cdot\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}x^{\prime}\in[S]^{d}\\
\mathrm{Ham}\left(x,x^{\prime}\right)=1\end{subarray}}\frac{q_{t}(x^{\prime})}{q_{t}(x)}+d\frac{S-1}{S}\lesssim dS\max\{1,t^{-1}\}.

Therefore,

\left|\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\left(\frac{q_{t}(y)}{q_{t}(x)}\right)\right|\lesssim dS^{2}\max\{1,t^{-2}\},

and thus

\left|\frac{q_{t}(y)}{q_{t}(x)}-\frac{q_{s}(y)}{q_{s}(x)}\right|\lesssim\left|\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\left(\frac{q_{t}(y)}{q_{t}(x)}\right)\right|(t-s)\lesssim dS^{2}\max\{1,t^{-2}\}(t-s),

as claimed. Especially note the quadratic dependency on
S
.

H.5
Proof of
Lemma

5

First, fix
x_{t}
and
y
and let
i
be the index such that
x_{t}^{i}\neq y^{i}
. From (
H.2
), we have,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{q_{t}(y)}{q_{t}(x_{t})}-\frac{q_{s}(y)}{q_{s}(x_{t})}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\mathbb{E}_{x_{0}\sim q_{0|t}(\cdot|x_{t})}\left[\frac{q^{i}_{t|0}(y^{i}|x_{0}^{i})}{q^{i}_{t|0}(x_{t}^{i}|x_{0}^{i})}\right]-\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{x}_{0}\sim q_{0|s}(\cdot|x_{t})}\left[\frac{q_{s|0}^{i}(y^{i}|\tilde{x}_{0}^{i})}{q_{s|0}^{i}(x_{t}^{i}|\tilde{x}_{0}^{i})}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{x_{0}\sim q_{0|t}(\cdot|x_{t})}\left|\frac{q^{i}_{t|0}(y^{i}|x_{0}^{i})}{q^{i}_{t|0}(x_{t}^{i}|x_{0}^{i})}-\frac{q_{s|0}^{i}(y^{i}|x_{0}^{i})}{q_{s|0}^{i}(x_{t}^{i}|x_{0}^{i})}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad+\left|\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}x_{0}\sim q_{0|t}(\cdot|x_{t})\\
\tilde{x}_{0}\sim q_{0|s}(\cdot|x_{t})\end{subarray}}\left[\frac{q_{s|0}^{i}(y^{i}|x_{0}^{i})}{q_{s|0}^{i}(x_{t}^{i}|x_{0}^{i})}-\frac{q_{s|0}^{i}(y^{i}|\tilde{x}_{0}^{i})}{q_{s|0}^{i}(x_{t}^{i}|\tilde{x}_{0}^{i})}\right]\right|.

(35)

For the first term in (
H.5
), we note the expression of likelihood ratio in (
34
) and thus, for any fixed
x_{0}
,
x_{t}
, and
y
,

\left|\frac{q^{i}_{t|0}(y^{i}|x_{0}^{i})}{q^{i}_{t|0}(x_{t}^{i}|x_{0}^{i})}-\frac{q_{s|0}^{i}(y^{i}|x_{0}^{i})}{q_{s|0}^{i}(x_{t}^{i}|x_{0}^{i})}\right|=\begin{cases}0&\text{if}\penalty\ x^{i}\neq x_{0}^{i}\penalty\ \text{and}\penalty\ y^{i}\neq x_{0}^{i}\\
\frac{1-e^{-t}}{1+(S-1)e^{-t}}-\frac{1-e^{-s}}{1+(S-1)e^{-s}}&\text{if}\penalty\ x^{i}=x_{0}^{i}\penalty\ \text{but}\penalty\ y^{i}\neq x_{0}^{i}\\
\frac{1+(S-1)e^{-t}}{1-e^{-t}}-\frac{1+(S-1)e^{-s}}{1-e^{-s}}&\text{if}\penalty\ x^{i}\neq x_{0}^{i}\penalty\ \text{but}\penalty\ y^{i}=x_{0}^{i}\end{cases}.

Now, since

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\left(\frac{1-e^{-t}}{1+(S-1)e^{-t}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{Se^{t}}{(S+e^{t}-1)^{2}}\lesssim 1

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\left(\frac{1+(S-1)e^{-t}}{1-e^{-t}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{Se^{t}}{(e^{t}-1)^{2}}\lesssim\frac{S}{\min\left\{1,t\right\}^{2}},

we have

\left|\frac{q^{i}_{t|0}(y^{i}|x_{0}^{i})}{q^{i}_{t|0}(x_{t}^{i}|x_{0}^{i})}-\frac{q_{s|0}^{i}(y^{i}|x_{0}^{i})}{q_{s|0}^{i}(x_{t}^{i}|x_{0}^{i})}\right|\lesssim\frac{S}{\min\left\{1,t\right\}^{2}}(t-s).

Note that this term does not depend on
d
. Thus,

\mathbb{E}_{x_{t}\sim q_{t}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\neq x_{t}\\
\mathrm{Ham}\left(y,x_{t}\right)=1\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}_{x_{0}\sim q_{0|t}(\cdot|x_{t})}\left|\frac{q^{i}_{t|0}(y^{i}|x_{0}^{i})}{q^{i}_{t|0}(x_{t}^{i}|x_{0}^{i})}-\frac{q_{s|0}^{i}(y^{i}|x_{0}^{i})}{q_{s|0}^{i}(x_{t}^{i}|x_{0}^{i})}\right|R_{t}(y,x_{t})\\
\lesssim dS\max\left\{1,t^{-2}\right\}(t-s).

Now we turn to the second term in (
H.5
). Write
f(z):=\frac{q_{s|0}^{i}(y^{i}|z)}{q_{s|0}^{i}(x_{t}^{i}|z)}
for brevity (recall that
x_{t}
and
y
are fixed and thus omitted in this expression). Note that from (
34
), an upper bound on
f(z)
is

\sup_{y^{i},x_{t}^{i},z\in[S]}f(z)=\sup_{y^{i},x_{t}^{i},z\in[S]}\frac{q_{s|0}^{i}(y^{i}|z)}{q_{s|0}^{i}(x_{t}^{i}|z)}\lesssim S\cdot\max\{1,s^{-1}\}.

Thus, the second term in (
H.5
) can be upper-bounded (for each
y^{i}
) as

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}x_{0}\sim q_{0|t}(\cdot|x_{t})\\
\tilde{x}_{0}\sim q_{0|s}(\cdot|x_{t})\end{subarray}}\left[f(x_{0}^{i})-f(\tilde{x}_{0}^{i})\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\sum_{x_{0}\in[S]^{d}}f(x_{0}^{i})(q_{0|t}(x_{0}|x_{t})-q_{0|s}(x_{0}|x_{t}))\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim S\max\{1,s^{-1}\}\sum_{x_{0}\in[S]^{d}}\left|q_{0|t}(x_{0}|x_{t})-q_{0|s}(x_{0}|x_{t})\right|.

(36)

Using Bayes’ rule, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{x_{0}\in[S]^{d}}\left|q_{0|t}(x_{0}|x_{t})-q_{0|s}(x_{0}|x_{t})\right|=\sum_{x_{0}\in[S]^{d}}q_{0}(x_{0})\left|\frac{q_{t|0}(x_{t}|x_{0})}{q_{t}(x_{t})}-\frac{q_{s|0}(x_{t}|x_{0})}{q_{s}(x_{t})}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{q_{t}(x_{t})\cdot q_{s}(x_{t})}\sum_{x_{0},y_{0}\in[S]^{d}}q_{0}(x_{0})q_{0}(y_{0})\left|q_{t|0}(x_{t}|x_{0})q_{s|0}(x_{t}|y_{0})-q_{s|0}(x_{t}|x_{0})q_{t|0}(x_{t}|y_{0})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{q_{t}(x_{t})\cdot q_{s}(x_{t})}\mathbb{E}_{x_{0},y_{0}\sim q_{0}}\bigg[\left|q_{t|0}(x_{t}|x_{0})-q_{s|0}(x_{t}|x_{0})\right|q_{s|0}(x_{t}|y_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left|q_{s|0}(x_{t}|y_{0})-q_{t|0}(x_{t}|y_{0})\right|q_{s|0}(x_{t}|x_{0})\bigg]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{q_{t}(x_{t})}\mathbb{E}_{x_{0}\sim q_{0}}\left|q_{t|0}(x_{t}|x_{0})-q_{s|0}(x_{t}|x_{0})\right|+\frac{1}{q_{t}(x_{t})}\mathbb{E}_{y_{0}\sim q_{0}}\left|q_{s|0}(x_{t}|y_{0})-q_{t|0}(x_{t}|y_{0})\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{q_{t}(x_{t})}\mathbb{E}_{x_{0}\sim q_{0}}\left|q_{t|0}(x_{t}|x_{0})-q_{s|0}(x_{t}|x_{0})\right|.

(37)

Now, this term (without the constant factor 2) can be upper-bounded as

\displaystyle\frac{1}{q_{t}(x_{t})}\mathbb{E}_{x_{0}\sim q_{0}}\left|q_{t|0}(x_{t}|x_{0})-q_{s|0}(x_{t}|x_{0})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{q_{t}(x_{t})}(t-s)\mathbb{E}_{x_{0}\sim q_{0}}\left|\frac{\partial}{\partial t}q_{t|0}(x_{t}|x_{0})\right|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{=}}\frac{1}{q_{t}(x_{t})}(t-s)\mathbb{E}_{x_{0}\sim q_{0}}\left|\sum_{\tilde{x}_{t}\in[S]^{d}}q_{t|0}(\tilde{x}_{t}|x_{0})R_{t}(\tilde{x}_{t},x_{t})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{q_{t}(x_{t})}(t-s)\mathbb{E}_{x_{0}\sim q_{0}}\sum_{\tilde{x}_{t}\in[S]^{d}}q_{t|0}(\tilde{x}_{t}|x_{0})\left|R_{t}(\tilde{x}_{t},x_{t})\right|

\displaystyle=(t-s)\sum_{\tilde{x}_{t}\in[S]^{d}}\frac{q_{t}(\tilde{x}_{t})}{q_{t}(x_{t})}\left|R_{t}(\tilde{x}_{t},x_{t})\right|,

(38)

where
(i)
follows from Kolmogorov forward equation. Thus, combining these intermediate results, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{x_{t}\sim q_{t}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}y\neq x_{t}\\
\mathrm{Ham}\left(y,x_{t}\right)=1\end{subarray}}\left|\mathbb{E}_{\begin{subarray}{c}x_{0}\sim q_{0|t}(\cdot|x_{t})\\
\tilde{x}_{0}\sim q_{0|s}(\cdot|x_{t})\end{subarray}}\left[f(x_{0}^{i})-f(\tilde{x}_{0}^{i})\right]\right|R_{t}(y,x_{t})

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(ii)}}{{\lesssim}}dS\max\{1,s^{-1}\}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{x_{t}\sim q_{t}}\left[\frac{1}{q_{t}(x_{t})}\mathbb{E}_{x_{0}\sim q_{0}}\left|q_{t|0}(x_{t}|x_{0})-q_{s|0}(x_{t}|x_{0})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(iii)}}{{\lesssim}}(t-s)dS\max\{1,s^{-1}\}\mathbb{E}_{x_{t}\sim q_{t}}\left[\sum_{\tilde{x}_{t}\in[S]^{d}}\frac{q_{t}(\tilde{x}_{t})}{q_{t}(x_{t})}\left|R_{t}(\tilde{x}_{t},x_{t})\right|\right]

\displaystyle=(t-s)dS\max\{1,s^{-1}\}\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{x}_{t}\sim q_{t}}\sum_{x_{t}\in[S]^{d}}\left|R_{t}(\tilde{x}_{t},x_{t})\right|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(iv)}}{{\asymp}}(t-s)d^{2}S\max\{1,s^{-1}\},

where
(ii)
follows from (
H.5
) and (
H.5
),
(iii)
follows from (
H.5
), and
(iv)
follows because
-R_{t}(x,x)=\sum_{y\neq x}R_{t}(x,y)=\frac{S-1}{S}d
for all
x,y\in[S]^{d}
. The proof is now complete.

H.6
Proof of
Lemma

6

Note that
\hat{R}^{\text{TTL}}_{t}(x,y)\equiv 0
whenever
\mathrm{Ham}\left(x,y\right)\geq 2
. Also recall the definition of the token-wise rate
\hat{R}_{k}^{i}
in (
9
). Note that
\hat{R}_{k}^{i}
is a valid rate matrix since
\sum_{a\in[S]}\hat{R}_{k}^{i}(z,a)=0
for all
z\in[S]
and
\hat{R}_{k}^{i}(z,a)\geq 0
if
z\neq a
.

Fix
x
and
y
such that
\mathrm{Ham}\left(x,y\right)=1
. Let
i^{*}\in[d]
be the (only) index such that
y^{i^{*}}\neq x^{i^{*}}
. We now divide into the following three cases:

1.

Case 1:
x=x_{t_{k}}
. Then,
\mathrm{zeros}(x-x_{t_{k}})=[S]
, and
\hat{R}^{\text{SE-tau-leap}}_{t}(x,y)=\hat{R}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},y)=\hat{R}_{k}^{i^{*}}(x_{t_{k}}^{i^{*}},y^{i^{*}})
.

2.

Case 2:
x\neq x_{t_{k}}
, but
x^{i^{*}}=x_{t_{k}}^{i^{*}}
. Thus,
i^{*}\in\mathrm{zeros}(x-x_{t_{k}})
, and
\hat{R}^{\text{SE-tau-leap}}_{t}(x,y)=\hat{R}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},x_{t_{k}}^{-i^{*}}\oplus_{i^{*}}y^{i^{*}})=\hat{R}_{k}^{i^{*}}(x_{t_{k}}^{i^{*}},y^{i^{*}})
.

3.

Case 3:
x^{i^{*}}\neq x_{t_{k}}^{i^{*}}
(and also
x\neq x_{t_{k}}
). Then,
i^{*}\notin\mathrm{zeros}(x-x_{t_{k}})
, and
\hat{R}^{\text{SE-tau-leap}}_{t}(x,y)=0
.

Thus, the overall CTMC is equivalent to
S
CTMC’s, one for each dimension. The transition rate matrix on the
i
-th dimension is
\hat{R}_{k}^{i}(z,a)\mathds{1}\left\{z=x_{t_{k}}^{i}\right\}
. Notably, at most one state transition can happen during
t\in[t_{k},t_{k+1})
(where the CTMC stops after its first transition). We can also calculate the corresponding state transition probability by solving the Kolmogorov forward equation, which is equal to (see
[
51
, Chap. 2]
for examples)

P^{i}_{t_{k+1}|t_{k}}\left\{x_{t_{k+1}}^{i}=a|x_{t_{k}}\right\}=\begin{cases}\exp\left(\hat{R}_{k}^{i}(x_{t_{k}}^{i},x_{t_{k}}^{i})(t_{k+1}-t_{k})\right)&\text{if}\penalty\ a=x_{t_{k}}^{i}\\
\frac{\hat{R}_{k}^{i}(x_{t_{k}}^{i},a)}{-\hat{R}_{k}^{i}(x_{t_{k}}^{i},x_{t_{k}}^{i})}\left(1-\exp\left(\hat{R}_{k}^{i}(x_{t_{k}}^{i},x_{t_{k}}^{i})(t_{k+1}-t_{k})\right)\right)&\text{if}\penalty\ a\neq x_{t_{k}}^{i}\end{cases}.

This is the same as the transition in (
23
). The proof is now complete.

H.7
Proof of
Lemma

7

Recall the conditional probabilities from (
23
), (
8
), and (
10
). Also there we have defined
\hat{R}_{k}^{i}
such that

\hat{R}_{k}^{i}(x_{t_{k}}^{i},a)=\hat{R}_{t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}},x_{t_{k}}^{-i}\oplus_{i}a),\quad\forall a\neq x_{t_{k}}^{i}.

As follows, we only consider all
a\in[S]
such that
a\neq x_{t_{k}}^{i}
, since all probability vectors need to sum up as 1.

We first focus on the Euler method. From (
23
),

\displaystyle P_{\text{truncated}}^{i}(a)

\displaystyle=\frac{\hat{R}_{k}^{i}(x_{t_{k}}^{i},a)}{-\hat{R}_{k}^{i}(x_{t_{k}}^{i},x_{t_{k}}^{i})}\left(1-\exp\left(\hat{R}_{k}^{i}(x_{t_{k}}^{i},x_{t_{k}}^{i})\cdot(t_{k+1}-t_{k})\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\hat{R}_{k}^{i}(x_{t_{k}}^{i},a)}{-\hat{R}_{k}^{i}(x_{t_{k}}^{i},x_{t_{k}}^{i})}(-\hat{R}_{k}^{i}(x_{t_{k}}^{i},x_{t_{k}}^{i})\cdot((t_{k+1}-t_{k})+o(t_{k+1}-t_{k})))

\displaystyle=\hat{R}_{k}^{i}(x_{t_{k}}^{i},a)(t_{k+1}-t_{k})(1+o(1))

\displaystyle=P_{\text{euler}}^{i}(a)(1+o(1)).

Also, for Tweedie
\tau
-leaping, from (
10
), we have

\displaystyle P_{\text{tweedie}}^{i}(a)

\displaystyle=\left(\left[e^{-(\bar{\beta}_{T-t_{k}}-\bar{\beta}_{T-t_{k+1}})R_{\text{base}}}\right]^{a:}s_{T-t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}}^{-i}\oplus_{i}\cdot,x_{t_{k}})\right)\left[e^{(\bar{\beta}_{T-t_{k}}-\bar{\beta}_{T-t_{k+1}})R_{\text{base}}}\right]^{a,x^{i}_{t_{k}}}

\displaystyle=\left[\delta_{a}-(\bar{\beta}_{T-t_{k}}-\bar{\beta}_{T-t_{k+1}})R_{\text{base}}(a,\cdot)\right]s_{T-t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}}^{-i}\oplus_{i}\cdot,x_{t_{k}})(\bar{\beta}_{T-t_{k}}-\bar{\beta}_{T-t_{k+1}})R_{\text{base}}(a,x^{i}_{t_{k}})(1+o(1))

\displaystyle=s_{T-t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}}^{-i}\oplus_{i}a,x_{t_{k}})(\bar{\beta}_{T-t_{k}}-\bar{\beta}_{T-t_{k+1}})R_{\text{base}}(a,x^{i}_{t_{k}})(1+o(1))

\displaystyle=s_{T-t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}}^{-i}\oplus_{i}a,x_{t_{k}})(t_{k+1}-t_{k})\beta_{T-t_{k}}R_{\text{base}}(a,x^{i}_{t_{k}})(1+o(1))

\displaystyle=s_{T-t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}}^{-i}\oplus_{i}a,x_{t_{k}})R_{T-t_{k}}(x_{t_{k}}^{-i}\oplus_{i}a,x_{t_{k}})(t_{k+1}-t_{k})(1+o(1))

\displaystyle=\hat{R}_{k}^{i}(x_{t_{k}}^{i},a)(t_{k+1}-t_{k})(1+o(1))

\displaystyle=P_{\text{euler}}^{i}(a)(1+o(1)),

where
\delta_{a}
is such that
\mathrm{Ham}\left(\delta_{a},0\right)=1
and
[\delta_{a}]^{a}=1
. The proof is now complete.

H.8
Lemma

8
and its proof

Lemma 8
.

Fix
a\in[S]^{d}
. We have

\lim_{s\downarrow t_{k}}\mathrm{KL}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{s|t_{k}}(\cdot|a)||p_{s|t_{k}}(\cdot|a))=0.

Proof of
Lemma

8
.

First, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{s\downarrow t_{k}}\mathrm{KL}(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{s|t_{k}}(\cdot|a)||p_{s|t_{k}}(\cdot|a))

\displaystyle=\lim_{s\downarrow t_{k}}\sum_{x\in\mathcal{X}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{s|t_{k}}(x|a)\log\frac{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{s|t_{k}}(x|a)}{p_{s|t_{k}}(x|a)}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(*)}}{{=}}\sum_{x\in\mathcal{X}}\lim_{s\downarrow t_{k}}\left(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{s|t_{k}}(x|a)\log\frac{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{s|t_{k}}(x|a)}{p_{s|t_{k}}(x|a)}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{x\in\mathcal{X}}\left(\lim_{s\downarrow t_{k}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{s|t_{k}}(x|a)\right)\left(\lim_{s\downarrow t_{k}}\log\frac{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{s|t_{k}}(x|a)}{p_{s|t_{k}}(x|a)}\right).

Here
(*)
follows because, different from the case where
\mathcal{X}=\mathbb{R}^{d}
, we can safely interchange the limit and summation because
\mathcal{X}
has finite cardinality. Now, by definition of the CTMC process (see (
1
)),

\lim_{s\downarrow t_{k}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{s|t_{k}}(x|a)=\lim_{s\downarrow t_{k}}p_{s|t_{k}}(x|a)=\mathds{1}\left\{x=a\right\},

which implies the desired result.
∎

H.9
Lemma

9
and its proof

Lemma 9
.

Fix
s<t
(thus
T-s>T-t
) and
a\in[S]^{d}
. We have

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x,y)=\frac{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t|s}(y|a)}{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t|s}(x|a)}R_{T-t|T-s}(y,x|a),\quad\forall x\neq y.

Here
R_{T-t|T-s}(\cdot,\cdot|a)
is defined as the rate matrix for the forward CTMC at time
T-t
conditioned on the future observation at time
T-s
being
a
. Indeed, we further have

R_{T-t|T-s}(y,x|a)=\frac{q_{T-s|T-t}(a|x)}{q_{T-s|T-t}(a|y)}R_{T-t}(y,x)\in[0,\infty).

Proof of
Lemma

9
.

Fix
x\neq y
. First, by Bayes’ rule,
\forall\tilde{t}\in(s,t)
(and thus
T-s>T-\tilde{t}>T-t
),

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t|\tilde{t},s}(y|x,a)=q_{T-t|T-\tilde{t},T-s}(y|x,a)=q_{T-\tilde{t}|T-t,T-s}(x|y,a)\cdot\frac{q_{T-t|T-s}(y|a)}{q_{T-\tilde{t}|T-s}(x|a)}.

For the left-hand side, by the Markov property of the reverse process, we have that

\lim_{\tilde{t}\to t}\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t|\tilde{t},s}(y|x,a)=\lim_{\tilde{t}\to t}\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t|\tilde{t}}(y|x)=\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x,y).

For the right-hand side, we note that

\displaystyle\lim_{\tilde{t}\to t}\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\left(q_{T-\tilde{t}|T-t,T-s}(x|y,a)\cdot\frac{q_{T-t|T-s}(y|a)}{q_{T-\tilde{t}|T-s}(x|a)}\right)

\displaystyle=\lim_{\tilde{t}\to t}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial t}q_{T-\tilde{t}|T-t,T-s}(x|y,a)\right)\frac{q_{T-t|T-s}(y|a)}{q_{T-\tilde{t}|T-s}(x|a)}+\lim_{\tilde{t}\to t}q_{T-\tilde{t}|T-t,T-s}(x|y,a)\frac{\frac{\partial}{\partial t}q_{T-t|T-s}(y|a)}{q_{T-\tilde{t}|T-s}(x|a)}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(i)}}{{=}}\lim_{\tilde{t}\to t}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial t}q_{T-\tilde{t}|T-t,T-s}(x|y,a)\right)\frac{q_{T-t|T-s}(y|a)}{q_{T-\tilde{t}|T-s}(x|a)}

\displaystyle=\left(\lim_{\tilde{t}\to t}\frac{\partial}{\partial t}q_{T-\tilde{t}|T-t,T-s}(x|y,a)\right)\frac{q_{T-t|T-s}(y|a)}{q_{T-t|T-s}(x|a)}

where
(i)
follows because
\lim_{\tilde{t}\to t}q_{T-\tilde{t}|T-t,T-s}(x|y,a)=0
for
x\neq y
. Also, by Kolmogorov backward equation,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial t}q_{T-\tilde{t}|T-t,T-s}(x|y,a)

\displaystyle=-\frac{\partial}{\partial(T-t)}q_{T-\tilde{t}|T-t,T-s}(x|y,a)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\tilde{x}\in[S]^{d}}q_{T-\tilde{t}|T-t,T-s}(\tilde{x}|y,a)R_{T-t|T-s}(\tilde{x},x|a),

where
R_{T-t|T-s}(\cdot,\cdot|a)
is the rate matrix for the forward CTMC at time
T-t
conditioned on the future event that the observation at time
T-s
is
a
. Then, combining both sides, we would get

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle R\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle R\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t}(x,y)=\frac{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t|s}(y|a)}{\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle q\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle q\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{t|s}(x|a)}R_{T-t|T-s}(y,x|a).

Lastly, we ensure that the conditioned rate is well-defined. Fix
s^{\prime}<t^{\prime}<T^{\prime}
. Note that

R_{t^{\prime}|T^{\prime}}(y,x|a)=\lim_{s^{\prime}\to t^{\prime}}\frac{\partial}{\partial t^{\prime}}q_{t^{\prime}|s^{\prime},T^{\prime}}(x|y,a).

Thus, we obviously have
R_{t^{\prime}|T^{\prime}}(y,x|a)=0
for all
x\neq y
if
q_{T^{\prime}|t^{\prime}}(a|y)=0
. Otherwise, we have

\displaystyle R_{t^{\prime}|T^{\prime}}(y,x|a)

\displaystyle=\lim_{s^{\prime}\to t^{\prime}}\frac{\partial}{\partial t^{\prime}}q_{t^{\prime}|s^{\prime},T^{\prime}}(x|y,a)

\displaystyle=\lim_{s^{\prime}\to t^{\prime}}\frac{1}{q_{T^{\prime}|s^{\prime}}(a|y)}\frac{\partial}{\partial t^{\prime}}\left(q_{T^{\prime}|s^{\prime},t^{\prime}}(a|y,x)q_{t^{\prime}|s^{\prime}}(x|y)\right)

\displaystyle=\lim_{s^{\prime}\to t^{\prime}}\frac{1}{q_{T^{\prime}|s^{\prime}}(a|y)}\frac{\partial}{\partial t^{\prime}}\left(q_{T^{\prime}|t^{\prime}}(a|x)q_{t^{\prime}|s^{\prime}}(x|y)\right)

\displaystyle=\lim_{s^{\prime}\to t^{\prime}}\frac{1}{q_{T^{\prime}|s^{\prime}}(a|y)}\left(-q_{t^{\prime}|s^{\prime}}(x|y)\sum_{\tilde{x}\in[S]^{d}}R_{t^{\prime}}(x,\tilde{x})q_{T^{\prime}|t^{\prime}}(a|\tilde{x})+q_{T^{\prime}|t^{\prime}}(a|x)\sum_{\tilde{x}\in[S]^{d}}q_{t^{\prime}|s^{\prime}}(\tilde{x}|y)R_{t^{\prime}}(\tilde{x},x)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{q_{T^{\prime}|t^{\prime}}(a|x)}{q_{T^{\prime}|t^{\prime}}(a|y)}R_{t^{\prime}}(y,x)

which is finite and non-negative when
x\neq y
. The proof is now complete.
∎
